   496→// NewCheckpointCommits returns the SHAs of commits added to the
   497→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch since the test was set up, oldest first.
   498→func NewCheckpointCommits(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) []string {
   499→	t.Helper()
   500→
   501→	log := GitOutput(t, s.Dir, "log", "--reverse", "--format=%H", s.CheckpointBefore+"..entire/checkpoints/v1")
   502→	if log == "" {
   503→		return nil
   504→	}
   505→	return strings.Split(log, "\n")
   506→}
   507→
   508→// CheckpointIDs lists all checkpoint IDs from the tree at the tip of the
   509→// checkpoint branch. It parses the two-level directory structure
   510→// ({prefix}/{suffix}/metadata.json) and returns the concatenated IDs.
   511→func CheckpointIDs(t *testing.T, dir string) []string {
   512→	t.Helper()
   513→	out := gitOutputSafe(dir, "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", "entire/checkpoints/v1")
   514→	if out == "" {
   515→		return nil
   516→	}
   517→	seen := map[string]bool{}
   518→	var ids []string
   519→	for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(out), "\n") {
   520→		// Match {prefix}/{suffix}/metadata.json (top-level, not session-level)
   521→		parts := strings.Split(line, "/")
   522→		if len(parts) == 3 && parts[2] == "metadata.json" {
   523→			id := parts[0] + parts[1]
   524→			if !seen[id] {
   525→				seen[id] = true
   526→				ids = append(ids, id)
   527→			}
   528→		}
   529→	}
   530→	return ids
   531→}
   532→
   533→// ReadCheckpointMetadata reads the checkpoint-level metadata.json from the
   534→// tip of the checkpoint branch for the given checkpoint ID.
   535→func ReadCheckpointMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string) CheckpointMetadata {
   536→	t.Helper()
   537→
   538→	path := CheckpointPath(checkpointID) + "/metadata.json"
   539→	blob := "entire/checkpoints/v1:" + path
   540→
   541→	raw := GitOutput(t, dir, "show", blob)
   542→
   543→	var meta CheckpointMetadata
   544→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {
   545→		t.Fatalf("unmarshal checkpoint metadata from %s: %v", blob, err)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	return meta
   549→}

   551→// ReadSessionMetadata reads a session's metadata.json from the tip of the
   552→// checkpoint branch for the given checkpoint ID and session index.
   553→func ReadSessionMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, sessionIndex int) SessionMetadata {
   554→	t.Helper()
   555→
   556→	path := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d/metadata.json", CheckpointPath(checkpointID), sessionIndex)
   557→	blob := "entire/checkpoints/v1:" + path
   558→
   559→	raw := GitOutput(t, dir, "show", blob)
   560→
   561→	var meta SessionMetadata
   562→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {

   564→	}
   565→
   566→	return meta

   568→
   569→// WaitForSessionMetadata polls until session metadata exists on the checkpoint
   570→// branch for the given checkpoint ID and session index, then returns it.
   571→// This handles the race where the checkpoint branch advances before session
   572→// metadata is fully committed.
   573→func WaitForSessionMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, sessionIndex int, timeout time.Duration) SessionMetadata {
   574→	t.Helper()
   575→
   576→	path := fmt.Sprintf("%s/%d/metadata.json", CheckpointPath(checkpointID), sessionIndex)
   577→	blob := "entire/checkpoints/v1:" + path
   578→
   579→	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
   580→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   581→		raw := gitOutputSafe(dir, "show", blob)
   582→		if raw != "" {
   583→			var meta SessionMetadata
   584→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {
   585→				t.Fatalf("unmarshal session metadata from %s: %v", blob, err)
   586→			}
   587→			return meta
   588→		}
   589→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
   590→	}
   591→	t.Fatalf("session metadata %s did not appear within %s", blob, timeout)
   592→	return SessionMetadata{}
   593→}
   594→
   595→// SetupBareRemote creates a bare git repo, adds it as "origin", and pushes
   596→// the initial commit. Returns the bare repo path.
   597→func SetupBareRemote(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) string {
   598→	t.Helper()
   599→
   600→	var bareDir string
   601→	if os.Getenv("E2E_KEEP_REPOS") != "" {
   602→		var err error
   603→		bareDir, err = os.MkdirTemp("", "e2e-bare-*")
   604→		if err != nil {
   605→			t.Fatalf("create bare remote dir: %v", err)
   606→		}
   607→		t.Logf("E2E_KEEP_REPOS: bare remote will be preserved at %s", bareDir)
   608→	} else {
   609→		bareDir = t.TempDir()
   610→	}
   611→
   612→	Git(t, bareDir, "init", "--bare")
   613→	Git(t, s.Dir, "remote", "add", "origin", bareDir)
   614→	Git(t, s.Dir, "push", "-u", "origin", "HEAD")
   615→	return bareDir
   616→}
   617→
   618→// GitOutputErr runs a git command and returns (output, error) without
   619→// failing the test. For commands expected to fail.
   620→func GitOutputErr(dir string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   621→	cmd := exec.Command("git", args...)
   622→	if dir != "" {
   623→		cmd.Dir = dir
   624→	}
   625→	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   626→	return strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), err
   627→}

   629→// GetCheckpointTrailer extracts the Entire-Checkpoint trailer value from a
   630→// code commit. Returns the trimmed trailer value, or an empty string if the

   632→func GetCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, dir string, ref string) string {
   633→	t.Helper()
   634→
   635→	return GitOutput(t, dir, "log", "-1", "--format=%(trailers:key=Entire-Checkpoint,valueonly)", ref)
   636→}

    30→// AssertFileExists asserts that at least one file matches the glob pattern
    31→// relative to dir.
    32→func AssertFileExists(t *testing.T, dir string, glob string) {
    33→	t.Helper()
    34→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(filepath.Join(dir, glob))
    35→	require.NoError(t, err)
    36→	assert.NotEmpty(t, matches, "expected files matching %s in %s", glob, dir)
    37→}
    38→
    39→// WaitForFileExists polls until at least one file matches the glob pattern
    40→// relative to dir, or fails the test after timeout. Handles the race where an
    41→// interactive agent's prompt pattern appears before file writes land on disk.
    42→func WaitForFileExists(t *testing.T, dir string, glob string, timeout time.Duration) {
    43→	t.Helper()
    44→	pattern := filepath.Join(dir, glob)
    45→	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
    46→	for {
    47→		matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
    48→		require.NoError(t, err)
    49→		if len(matches) > 0 {
    50→			return
    51→		}
    52→		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
    53→			t.Fatalf("expected files matching %s in %s within %s", glob, dir, timeout)
    54→		}
    55→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// AssertNewCommits polls until at least `atLeast` new commits exist since setup,
    60→// or fails after 20 seconds. Polling handles the race where an interactive
    61→// agent's prompt pattern appears before its git commit lands on disk.
    62→func AssertNewCommits(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, atLeast int) {
    63→	t.Helper()
    64→	deadline := time.Now().Add(20 * time.Second)
    65→	for {
    66→		out := GitOutput(t, s.Dir, "log", "--oneline", s.HeadBefore+"..HEAD")
    67→		var lines []string
    68→		if out != "" {
    69→			lines = strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(out), "\n")
    70→		}
    71→		if len(lines) >= atLeast {
    72→			return
    73→		}
    74→		if time.Now().After(deadline) {
    75→			t.Fatalf("expected at least %d new commit(s), got %d after 20s", atLeast, len(lines))
    76→		}
    77→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
    78→	}
    79→}
    80→
    81→// WaitForCheckpoint polls until the checkpoint branch advances from its
    82→// initial state, or fails the test after timeout. Use this before any
    83→// assertions that depend on the checkpoint branch (post-commit hook is async).
    84→func WaitForCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, timeout time.Duration) {
    85→	t.Helper()
    86→	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
    87→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
    88→		after := GitOutput(t, s.Dir, "rev-parse", "entire/checkpoints/v1")
    89→		if after != s.CheckpointBefore {
    90→			return
    91→		}
    92→		time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
    93→	}
    94→	t.Fatalf("checkpoint branch did not advance within %s", timeout)
    95→}

    97→// shadowBranches returns all shadow branches (entire/*) excluding entire/checkpoints/*.
    98→func shadowBranches(t *testing.T, dir string) []string {
    99→	t.Helper()
   100→	branches := GitOutput(t, dir, "for-each-ref", "--format=%(refname:short)", "refs/heads/entire/")
   101→	var shadow []string
   102→	for _, b := range strings.Split(branches, "\n") {
   103→		b = strings.TrimSpace(b)
   104→		if b == "" || strings.HasPrefix(b, "entire/checkpoints") {
   105→			continue
   106→		}
   107→		shadow = append(shadow, b)
   108→	}
   109→	return shadow
   110→}

   216→	assert.NotEmpty(t, meta.CLIVersion, "checkpoint %s: cli_version should be set", checkpointID)
   217→	assert.NotEmpty(t, meta.Strategy, "checkpoint %s: strategy should be set", checkpointID)
   218→	assert.NotEmpty(t, meta.Sessions, "checkpoint %s: should have at least 1 session", checkpointID)
   219→	assert.Equal(t, checkpointID, meta.CheckpointID,
   220→		"checkpoint metadata ID should match expected")
   221→}
   222→
   223→// WaitForCheckpointAdvanceFrom polls until the checkpoint branch advances from
   224→// the given ref, or fails the test after timeout. Use this when waiting for a
   225→// second (or subsequent) checkpoint after recording the branch position.
   226→func WaitForCheckpointAdvanceFrom(t *testing.T, dir string, fromRef string, timeout time.Duration) {
   227→	t.Helper()
   228→	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
   229→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   230→		after := GitOutput(t, dir, "rev-parse", "entire/checkpoints/v1")
   231→		if after != fromRef {
   232→			return
   233→		}
   234→		time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
   235→	}
   236→	t.Fatalf("checkpoint branch did not advance from %s within %s", fromRef[:8], timeout)
   237→}
   238→
   239→// WaitForSessionIdle polls the session state files in .git/entire-sessions/
   240→// until no session has phase "active", or fails the test after timeout.
   241→// This handles the race where an agent's prompt pattern appears in the TUI
   242→// before the turn-end hook has completed (transitioning ACTIVE → IDLE).
   243→func WaitForSessionIdle(t *testing.T, dir string, timeout time.Duration) {
   244→	t.Helper()
   245→	stateDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".git", "entire-sessions")
   246→	deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
   247→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   248→		entries, err := os.ReadDir(stateDir)
   249→		if err != nil {
   250→			// Directory may not exist yet; keep polling
   251→			time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
   252→			continue
   253→		}
   254→		anyActive := false
   255→		for _, entry := range entries {
   256→			if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") || strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   257→				continue
   258→			}
   259→			data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(stateDir, entry.Name()))
   260→			if err != nil {
   261→				continue
   262→			}
   263→			var state struct {
   264→				Phase string `json:"phase"`
   265→			}
   266→			if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   267→				continue
   268→			}
   269→			if state.Phase == "active" {
   270→				anyActive = true
   271→				break
   272→			}
   273→		}
   274→		if !anyActive {
   275→			return
   276→		}
   277→		time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
   278→	}
   279→	t.Fatalf("session(s) did not transition to idle within %s", timeout)
   280→}
   281→
   282→// AssertNoCheckpointTrailer asserts the commit does NOT have an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   283→func AssertNoCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, dir string, ref string) {
   284→	t.Helper()
   285→	trailer := GetCheckpointTrailer(t, dir, ref)
   286→	assert.Empty(t, trailer, "expected no Entire-Checkpoint trailer on %s, got %q", ref, trailer)
   287→}
   288→
   289→// AssertCheckpointFilesTouched asserts the checkpoint metadata lists exactly
   290→// the expected files in files_touched (order-independent).
   291→func AssertCheckpointFilesTouched(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, expected []string) {
   292→	t.Helper()
   293→	meta := ReadCheckpointMetadata(t, dir, checkpointID)
   294→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, expected, meta.FilesTouched,
   295→		"checkpoint %s: files_touched mismatch", checkpointID)
   296→}
   297→
   298→// AssertCheckpointFilesTouchedContains asserts files_touched contains a
   299→// specific file path (subset check, unlike the exact-match AssertCheckpointFilesTouched).
   300→func AssertCheckpointFilesTouchedContains(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, file string) {
   301→	t.Helper()
   302→	meta := ReadCheckpointMetadata(t, dir, checkpointID)
   303→	assert.Contains(t, meta.FilesTouched, file,
   304→		"checkpoint %s: files_touched should contain %s", checkpointID, file)
   305→}
   306→
   307→// AssertDistinctSessions asserts session metadata across checkpoints has unique session IDs.
   308→func AssertDistinctSessions(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointIDs []string) {
   309→	t.Helper()
   310→	seen := map[string]bool{}
   311→	for _, cpID := range checkpointIDs {
   312→		sm := ReadSessionMetadata(t, dir, cpID, 0)
   313→		assert.False(t, seen[sm.SessionID],
   314→			"duplicate session_id %s across checkpoints", sm.SessionID)
   315→		seen[sm.SessionID] = true
   316→	}
   317→}
   318→
   319→// ValidateCheckpointDeep performs comprehensive validation of checkpoint metadata
   320→// on the checkpoint branch, including transcript JSONL validity, content hash
   321→// verification, and prompt content checking.
   322→func ValidateCheckpointDeep(t *testing.T, dir string, v DeepCheckpointValidation) {
   323→	t.Helper()
   324→
   325→	// Basic metadata validation
   326→	AssertCheckpointExists(t, dir, v.CheckpointID)
   327→	AssertCheckpointMetadataComplete(t, dir, v.CheckpointID)
   328→
   329→	if v.Strategy != "" {
   330→		meta := ReadCheckpointMetadata(t, dir, v.CheckpointID)
   331→		assert.Equal(t, v.Strategy, meta.Strategy,
   332→			"checkpoint %s: strategy mismatch", v.CheckpointID)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   336→		AssertCheckpointFilesTouched(t, dir, v.CheckpointID, v.FilesTouched)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	path := CheckpointPath(v.CheckpointID)
   340→
   341→	// Validate session metadata exists and has checkpoint_id
   342→	sessionBlob := fmt.Sprintf("entire/checkpoints/v1:%s/0/metadata.json", path)
   343→	sessionRaw := gitOutputSafe(dir, "show", sessionBlob)
   344→	if assert.NotEmpty(t, sessionRaw, "session metadata should exist at %s", sessionBlob) {
   345→		var sessionMeta map[string]any
   346→		if assert.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionRaw), &sessionMeta)) {
   347→			assert.Equal(t, v.CheckpointID, sessionMeta["checkpoint_id"],
   348→				"session metadata checkpoint_id should match")
   349→			assert.NotEmpty(t, sessionMeta["created_at"], "session metadata should have created_at")
   350→		}
   351→	}
   352→
   353→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL
   354→	transcriptBlob := fmt.Sprintf("entire/checkpoints/v1:%s/0/full.jsonl", path)
   355→	transcriptRaw := gitOutputSafe(dir, "show", transcriptBlob)
   356→	if assert.NotEmpty(t, transcriptRaw, "transcript should exist at %s", transcriptBlob) {
   357→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptRaw, "\n")
   358→		nonEmpty := 0
   359→		for _, line := range lines {
   360→			if strings.TrimSpace(line) != "" {
   361→				nonEmpty++
   362→			}
   363→		}
   364→		assert.Positive(t, nonEmpty, "transcript should have at least one line")
   365→
   366→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
   367→			assert.Contains(t, transcriptRaw, expected,
   368→				"transcript should contain %q", expected)
   369→		}
   370→
   371→		// Validate content hash
   372→		hashBlob := fmt.Sprintf("entire/checkpoints/v1:%s/0/content_hash.txt", path)
   373→		hashRaw := gitOutputSafe(dir, "show", hashBlob)
   374→		if hashRaw != "" {
   375→			hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptRaw))
   376→			expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   377→			assert.Equal(t, expectedHash, strings.TrimSpace(hashRaw),
   378→				"content hash should match transcript SHA-256")
   379→		}
   380→	}
   381→
   382→	// Validate prompt.txt if expected prompts specified
   383→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
   384→		promptBlob := fmt.Sprintf("entire/checkpoints/v1:%s/0/prompt.txt", path)
   385→		promptRaw := gitOutputSafe(dir, "show", promptBlob)
   386→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
   387→			assert.Contains(t, promptRaw, expected,
   388→				"prompt.txt should contain %q", expected)
   389→		}
   390→	}
   391→}
   392→

     1→package agents
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"errors"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"os/exec"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"syscall"
    12→	"time"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// cleanConfigDir creates an isolated temp directory for CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR so
    16→// that E2E test runs don't inherit any user settings (CLAUDE.md, skills,
    17→// projects, plugins, etc.).
    18→//
    19→// On CI, it symlinks .claude.json (which Bootstrap() wrote with the API key
    20→// and hasCompletedOnboarding). Locally, it writes a minimal .claude.json to
    21→// skip the onboarding flow — Keychain-based auth works without any other files.
    22→func cleanConfigDir() (string, error) {
    23→	dst, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "claude-config-*")
    24→	if err != nil {
    25→		return "", err
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	if os.Getenv("CI") != "" {
    29→		if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {
    30→			src := filepath.Join(home, ".claude", ".claude.json")
    31→			if _, err := os.Stat(src); err == nil {
    32→				_ = os.Symlink(src, filepath.Join(dst, ".claude.json"))
    33→			}
    34→		}
    35→	} else {
    36→		_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dst, ".claude.json"),
    37→			[]byte(`{"hasCompletedOnboarding":true}`), 0o644)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	return dst, nil
    41→}
    42→
    43→// cleanEnv returns os.Environ() with agent-incompatible variables removed.
    44→// It strips CLAUDECODE (so Claude Code doesn't refuse to start inside this
    45→// test runner) and ENTIRE_TEST_TTY (so agents exercise the real TTY detection
    46→// paths instead of the test override).
    47→func cleanEnv() []string {
    48→	var env []string
    49→	for _, e := range os.Environ() {
    50→		if strings.HasPrefix(e, "CLAUDECODE=") || strings.HasPrefix(e, "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=") {
    51→			continue
    52→		}
    53→		env = append(env, e)
    54→	}
    55→	return env
    56→}
    57→
    58→func init() {
    59→	if env := os.Getenv("E2E_AGENT"); env != "" && env != "claude-code" {
    60→		return
    61→	}
    62→	Register(&Claude{})
    63→}
    64→
    65→type Claude struct{}
    66→
    67→func (c *Claude) Name() string               { return "claude-code" }
    68→func (c *Claude) Binary() string             { return "claude" }
    69→func (c *Claude) EntireAgent() string        { return "claude-code" }
    70→func (c *Claude) PromptPattern() string      { return `❯` }
    71→func (c *Claude) TimeoutMultiplier() float64 { return 1.0 }
    72→
    73→func (c *Claude) IsTransientError(out Output, _ error) bool {
    74→	transientPatterns := []string{
    75→		"overloaded",
    76→		"rate limit",
    77→		"529",
    78→		"503",
    79→		"ECONNRESET",
    80→		"ETIMEDOUT",
    81→	}
    82→	for _, p := range transientPatterns {
    83→		if strings.Contains(out.Stderr, p) {
    84→			return true
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→	return false
    88→}
    89→
    90→func (c *Claude) Bootstrap() error {
    91→	// On CI, write a config file so Claude Code uses the API key from the
    92→	// environment instead of trying OAuth/Keychain.
    93→	if os.Getenv("CI") == "" {
    94→		return nil
    95→	}
    96→	apiKey := os.Getenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
    97→	if apiKey == "" {
    98→		return nil
    99→	}
   100→	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("get home dir: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→	dir := filepath.Join(home, ".claude")
   105→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   106→		return fmt.Errorf("mkdir %s: %w", dir, err)
   107→	}
   108→	config := fmt.Sprintf(`{"primaryApiKey":%q,"hasCompletedOnboarding":true}`, apiKey)
   109→	path := filepath.Join(dir, ".claude.json")
   110→	return os.WriteFile(path, []byte(config), 0o644)
   111→}
   112→
   113→func (c *Claude) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error) {
   114→	cfg := &runConfig{Model: "haiku"}
   115→	for _, o := range opts {
   116→		o(cfg)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	configDir, err := cleanConfigDir()
   120→	if err != nil {
   121→		return Output{}, fmt.Errorf("create clean config dir: %w", err)
   122→	}
   123→	defer func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(configDir) }()
   124→
   125→	env := append(cleanEnv(),
   126→		"ACCESSIBLE=1",
   127→
   128→		// See https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings - without this setting Claude was going off and
   129→		// trying to Git-clone its plugin marketplace, which meant calling git commands that could fail
   130→		// due to a user's exotic config (e.g. in paul's case, needing SSH-keychain access granted every
   131→		// time).  That's no good, so for the E2E tests, we tell Claude not to make calls to auto-update
   132→		// itself, clone its plugins, etc.
   133→		"CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1",
   134→		"CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR="+configDir,
   135→	)
   136→
   137→	args := []string{"-p", prompt, "--model", cfg.Model, "--dangerously-skip-permissions"}
   138→	displayArgs := []string{"-p", fmt.Sprintf("%q", prompt), "--model", cfg.Model, "--dangerously-skip-permissions"}
   139→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, c.Binary(), args...)
   140→	cmd.Dir = dir
   141→	cmd.Stdin = nil
   142→	cmd.Env = env
   143→	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}
   144→	cmd.Cancel = func() error {
   145→		return syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)
   146→	}
   147→	cmd.WaitDelay = 5 * time.Second
   148→
   149→	var stdout, stderr strings.Builder
   150→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   151→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   152→
   153→	err = cmd.Run()
   154→	exitCode := 0
   155→	if err != nil {
   156→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   157→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   158→			exitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   159→		} else {
   160→			exitCode = -1
   161→		}
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	return Output{
   165→		Command:  c.Binary() + " " + strings.Join(displayArgs, " "),
   166→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   167→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   168→		ExitCode: exitCode,
   169→	}, err
   170→}
   171→
   172→func (c *Claude) StartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error) {
   173→	name := fmt.Sprintf("claude-test-%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
   174→
   175→	configDir, err := cleanConfigDir()
   176→	if err != nil {
   177→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("create clean config dir: %w", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	envArgs := []string{
   181→		"ACCESSIBLE=1",
   182→
   183→		// See https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings - without this setting Claude was going off and
   184→		// trying to Git-clone its plugin marketplace, which meant calling git commands that could fail
   185→		// due to a user's exotic config (e.g. in paul's case, needing SSH-keychain access granted every
   186→		// time).  That's no good, so for the E2E tests, we tell Claude not to make calls to auto-update
   187→		// itself, clone its plugins, etc.
   188→		"CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1",
   189→		"CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=" + configDir,
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	args := append([]string{"env"}, envArgs...)
   193→	args = append(args, c.Binary(), "--dangerously-skip-permissions")
   194→	s, err := NewTmuxSession(name, dir, []string{"CLAUDECODE", "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"}, args[0], args[1:]...)
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		_ = os.RemoveAll(configDir)
   197→		return nil, err
   198→	}
   199→	s.OnClose(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(configDir) })
   200→
   201→	// Dismiss startup dialogs until we reach the input prompt.
   202→	for range 5 {
   203→		content, err := s.WaitFor(`❯`, 15*time.Second)
   204→		if err != nil {
   205→			_ = s.Close()
   206→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("waiting for startup prompt: %w", err)
   207→		}
   208→		if !strings.Contains(content, "Enter to confirm") {
   209→			break
   210→		}
   211→		// The bypass permissions dialog defaults to "No, exit" —
   212→		// arrow down to "Yes, I accept" before confirming.
   213→		if strings.Contains(content, "Yes, I accept") {
   214→			_ = s.SendKeys("Down")
   215→			time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
   216→		}
   217→		_ = s.SendKeys("Enter")
   218→		time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
   219→	}
   220→	s.stableAtSend = ""
   221→
   222→	return s, nil
   223→}
   224→

    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/e2e/testutil"
    16→)
    17→
    18→func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
    19→	runDir := os.Getenv("E2E_ARTIFACT_DIR")
    20→	if runDir == "" {
    21→		_, file, _, _ := runtime.Caller(0)
    22→		testutil.ArtifactRoot = filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(file), "..", "artifacts")
    23→		runDir = testutil.ArtifactRunDir()
    24→	}
    25→	_ = os.MkdirAll(runDir, 0o755)
    26→	testutil.SetRunDir(runDir)
    27→
    28→	// Resolve the entire binary (set by mise run build via E2E_ENTIRE_BIN).
    29→	entireBin := entire.BinPath()
    30→
    31→	// Prepend the binary's directory to PATH so that git hooks and agent
    32→	// hooks (which call bare "entire") resolve to the same binary the test
    33→	// harness uses, not a system-installed one.
    34→	os.Setenv("PATH", filepath.Dir(entireBin)+string(os.PathListSeparator)+os.Getenv("PATH"))
    35→
    36→	// Preflight: verify required dependencies before running any tests.
    37→	var missing []string
    38→	for _, bin := range []string{"git", "tmux"} {
    39→		if _, err := exec.LookPath(bin); err != nil {
    40→			missing = append(missing, bin)
    41→		}
    42→	}
    43→	for _, a := range agents.All() {
    44→		if _, err := exec.LookPath(a.Binary()); err != nil {
    45→			missing = append(missing, fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s)", a.Binary(), a.Name()))
    46→		}
    47→	}
    48→	if len(missing) > 0 {
    49→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "preflight: missing required binaries: %v\n", missing)
    50→		os.Exit(1)
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	version := "unknown"
    54→	if out, err := exec.Command(entireBin, "version").Output(); err == nil {
    55→		version = string(out)
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Write preflight info to artifact dir only — gotestsum swallows both
    59→	// stdout and stderr, so the test-e2e script cats this file at the end.
    60→	preflight := fmt.Sprintf("entire binary:  %s\nentire version: %s\n",
    61→		entireBin, version)
    62→	_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(runDir, "entire-version.txt"), []byte(preflight), 0o644)
    63→
    64→	// Don't look at user's Git config, ignore everything except the project-local Git settings.
    65→	// This avoids oddball configs in ~/.gitconfig messing with our E2E tests.
    66→	os.Setenv("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
    67→
    68→	os.Exit(m.Run())
    69→}
    70→

> AGENT

Now I have a complete picture. Let me implement this systematically. I'll start with Phase 1 (creating sub-packages) and the testify dependency, then proceed through the remaining phases.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Architecture pattern being ported:** The CLI repo separates concerns into `agents/` (agent interface + implementations), `entire/` (CLI wrapper), `testutil/` (repo setup + assertions + artifacts), and `tests/` (actual test files). This enables the `ForEachAgent` pattern where every lifecycle test automatically runs against all registered agents in parallel, with concurrency gating and transient error recovery.

**Key difference:** The CLI repo's tests live in a separate `tests` package inside the main module, while external-agents has its own `go.mod` at `e2e/`. This means we can keep tests in the root `e2e` package and import sub-packages directly.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

{

  "content": "package agents\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// Output holds the result of running an agent prompt.\ntype Output struct {\n\tCommand  string\n\tStdout   string\n\tStderr   string\n\tExitCode int\n}\n\n// Option configures how an agent prompt is executed.\ntype Option func(*runConfig)\ntype runConfig struct {\n\tModel          string\n\tPermissionMode string\n\tPromptTimeout  time.Duration\n}\n\n// WithModel sets the model to use for the prompt.\nfunc WithModel(model string) Option {\n\treturn func(c *runConfig) { c.Model = model }\n}\n\n// WithPermissionMode sets the permission mode for the prompt.\nfunc WithPermissionMode(mode string) Option {\n\treturn func(c *runConfig) { c.PermissionMode = mode }\n}\n\n// WithPromptTimeout sets a custom timeout for the prompt execution.\nfunc WithPromptTimeout(d time.Duration) Option {\n\treturn func(c *runConfig) { c.PromptTimeout = d }\n}\n\n// Agent defines the interface for an AI coding agent that can be tested.\ntype Agent interface {\n\tName() string\n\t// Binary returns the CLI binary name (e.g. \"kiro-cli-chat\").\n\tBinary() string\n\tEntireAgent() string\n\tPromptPattern() string\n\t// TimeoutMultiplier returns a factor applied to per-test timeouts.\n\t// Slower agents return values > 1.\n\tTimeoutMultiplier() float64\n\tRunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error)\n\tStartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error)\n\t// Bootstrap performs one-time CI setup (auth config, warmup, etc.).\n\t// Called before any tests run. Implementations should be idempotent.\n\tBootstrap() error\n\t// IsTransientError returns true if the error from RunPrompt looks like\n\t// a transient API failure (e.g. 500, rate limit, network error) that\n\t// is worth retrying.\n\tIsTransientError(out Output, err error) bool\n}\n\n// Session represents an interactive terminal session with an agent.\ntype Session interface {\n\tSend(input string) error\n\tWaitFor(pattern string, timeout time.Duration) (string, error)\n\tCapture() string\n\tClose() error\n}\n\n// ExternalAgent is an optional interface for agents discovered via the\n// external agent protocol (entire-agent-* binaries). SetupRepo uses this\n// to pre-configure external_agents in settings before running `entire enable`.\ntype ExternalAgent interface {\n\tIsExternalAgent() bool\n}\n\nvar registry []Agent\nvar gates = map[string]chan struct{}{}\n\n// Register adds an agent to the global registry.\nfunc Register(a Agent) {\n\tregistry = append(registry, a)\n}\n\n// RegisterGate sets a concurrency limit for an agent's tests.\n// Tests call AcquireSlot/ReleaseSlot to respect this limit.\n// The limit can be overridden via E2E_CONCURRENT_TEST_LIMIT.\nfunc RegisterGate(name string, defaultMax int) {\n\tmax := defaultMax\n\tif v, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv(\"E2E_CONCURRENT_TEST_LIMIT\")); err == nil && v > 0 {\n\t\tmax = v\n\t}\n\tgates[name] = make(chan struct{}, max)\n}\n\n// AcquireSlot blocks until a test slot is available for the agent or the\n// context is cancelled. Returns a non-nil error if the context expires\n// before a slot opens.\nfunc AcquireSlot(ctx context.Context, a Agent) error {\n\tg, ok := gates[a.Name()]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tselect {\n\tcase g <- struct{}{}:\n\t\treturn nil\n\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\treturn ctx.Err()\n\t}\n}\n\n// ReleaseSlot frees a test slot for the agent.\nfunc ReleaseSlot(a Agent) {\n\tif g, ok := gates[a.Name()]; ok {\n\t\t<-g\n\t}\n}\n\n// All returns all registered agents.\nfunc All() []Agent {\n\treturn registry\n}\n\n// filterEnv returns env with entries matching any of the given variable names\n// removed. Used to strip test-only overrides from agent processes.\nfunc filterEnv(env []string, names ...string) []string {\n\tout := make([]string, 0, len(env))\n\tfor _, e := range env {\n\t\tskip := false\n\t\tfor _, name := range names {\n\t\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(e, name+\"=\") {\n\t\t\t\tskip = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !skip {\n\t\t\tout = append(out, e)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package agents\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// TmuxSession implements Session using tmux for PTY-based interactive agents.\ntype TmuxSession struct {\n\tname         string\n\tstableAtSend string   // stable content snapshot when Send was last called\n\tcleanups     []func() // run on Close\n}\n\n// OnClose registers a function to run when the session is closed.\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) OnClose(fn func()) {\n\ts.cleanups = append(s.cleanups, fn)\n}\n\n// NewTmuxSession creates a new tmux session running the given command in dir.\n// unsetEnv lists environment variable names to strip from the session.\n//\n// The command is wrapped with `env` to propagate PATH from the current process.\n// tmux sessions inherit the tmux server's environment (not the client's), so\n// without this, binaries added to PATH by the test runner would not be found.\nfunc NewTmuxSession(name string, dir string, unsetEnv []string, command string, args ...string) (*TmuxSession, error) {\n\ts := &TmuxSession{name: name}\n\n\ttmuxArgs := []string{\"new-session\", \"-d\", \"-s\", name, \"-c\", dir}\n\tvar parts []string\n\tparts = append(parts, \"env\")\n\t// Options (-u) must precede variable assignments for BSD env on macOS.\n\tfor _, v := range unsetEnv {\n\t\tparts = append(parts, \"-u\", shellQuote(v))\n\t}\n\tparts = append(parts, \"PATH=\"+shellQuote(os.Getenv(\"PATH\")))\n\tparts = append(parts, shellQuote(command))\n\tfor _, a := range args {\n\t\tparts = append(parts, shellQuote(a))\n\t}\n\ttmuxArgs = append(tmuxArgs, strings.Join(parts, \" \"))\n\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", tmuxArgs...)\n\tif out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"tmux new-session: %w\\n%s\", err, out)\n\t}\n\t// Keep the pane around after the command exits so we can capture error output.\n\tsetCmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", \"set-option\", \"-t\", name, \"remain-on-exit\", \"on\")\n\t_ = setCmd.Run()\n\treturn s, nil\n}\n\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) Send(input string) error {\n\tpreSend := stableContent(s.Capture())\n\t// Send text and Enter separately — some TUIs can swallow Enter\n\t// if it arrives before the input handler finishes processing the text.\n\tif err := s.SendKeys(input); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\ttime.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)\n\tif err := s.SendKeys(\"Enter\"); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for the terminal to reflect the echoed input, then snapshot.\n\tdeadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)\n\tfor time.Now().Before(deadline) {\n\t\ttime.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)\n\t\tcurrent := stableContent(s.Capture())\n\t\tif current != preSend {\n\t\t\ts.stableAtSend = current\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\ts.stableAtSend = stableContent(s.Capture())\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// SendKeys sends raw tmux key names without appending Enter.\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) SendKeys(keys ...string) error {\n\targs := append([]string{\"send-keys\", \"-t\", s.name}, keys...)\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", args...)\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"tmux send-keys: %w\\n%s\", err, out)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nconst (\n\tsettleTime   = 2 * time.Second\n\tpollInterval = 500 * time.Millisecond\n)\n\n// stableContent returns the content with the last few lines stripped,\n// so that TUI status bar updates don't prevent the settle timer.\nfunc stableContent(content string) string {\n\tlines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\tif len(lines) > 3 {\n\t\tlines = lines[:len(lines)-3]\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Join(lines, \"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) WaitFor(pattern string, timeout time.Duration) (string, error) {\n\tre, err := regexp.Compile(pattern)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"invalid pattern: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tdeadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)\n\tvar matchedAt time.Time\n\tvar lastStable string\n\tcontentChanged := s.stableAtSend == \"\" // skip change requirement for initial waits\n\n\tfor time.Now().Before(deadline) {\n\t\tcontent := s.Capture()\n\t\tstable := stableContent(content)\n\n\t\t// Bail early if the process has exited and the pattern doesn't match.\n\t\tif !re.MatchString(content) {\n\t\t\tif s.IsPaneDead() {\n\t\t\t\treturn content, fmt.Errorf(\"process exited while waiting for %q\\n--- pane content ---\\n%s\\n--- end pane content ---\", pattern, content)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmatchedAt = time.Time{}\n\t\t\tlastStable = \"\"\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif !contentChanged && stable != s.stableAtSend {\n\t\t\tcontentChanged = true\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif stable != lastStable {\n\t\t\tmatchedAt = time.Now()\n\t\t\tlastStable = stable\n\t\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif contentChanged && time.Since(matchedAt) >= settleTime {\n\t\t\treturn content, nil\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttime.Sleep(pollInterval)\n\t}\n\tcontent := s.Capture()\n\treturn content, fmt.Errorf(\"timed out waiting for %q after %s\\n--- pane content ---\\n%s\\n--- end pane content ---\", pattern, timeout, content)\n}\n\n// IsPaneDead returns true if the process inside the tmux pane has exited.\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) IsPaneDead() bool {\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", \"display-message\", \"-t\", s.name, \"-p\", \"#{pane_dead}\")\n\tout, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn true\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(out)) == \"1\"\n}\n\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) Capture() string {\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", \"capture-pane\", \"-t\", s.name, \"-p\")\n\tout, _ := cmd.Output()\n\treturn strings.TrimRight(string(out), \"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (s *TmuxSession) Close() error {\n\tfor _, fn := range s.cleanups {\n\t\tfn()\n\t}\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"tmux\", \"kill-session\", \"-t\", s.name)\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"tmux kill-session: %w\\n%s\", err, out)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// shellQuote wraps s in single quotes with proper escaping for POSIX shells.\nfunc shellQuote(s string) string {\n\treturn \"'\" + strings.ReplaceAll(s, \"'\", \"'\\\\''\") + \"'\"\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package agents\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\nfunc init() {\n\tif env := os.Getenv(\"E2E_AGENT\"); env != \"\" && env != \"kiro\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRegister(&Kiro{})\n\tRegisterGate(\"kiro\", 2)\n}\n\n// Kiro implements Agent for the Kiro CLI (kiro-cli-chat).\ntype Kiro struct{}\n\nfunc (k *Kiro) Name() string               { return \"kiro\" }\nfunc (k *Kiro) Binary() string             { return \"kiro-cli-chat\" }\nfunc (k *Kiro) EntireAgent() string        { return \"kiro\" }\nfunc (k *Kiro) PromptPattern() string      { return `>` }\nfunc (k *Kiro) TimeoutMultiplier() float64 { return 1.0 }\nfunc (k *Kiro) IsExternalAgent() bool      { return true }\n\nfunc (k *Kiro) IsTransientError(out Output, _ error) bool {\n\tcombined := out.Stdout + out.Stderr\n\ttransientPatterns := []string{\n\t\t\"overloaded\",\n\t\t\"rate limit\",\n\t\t\"529\",\n\t\t\"503\",\n\t\t\"500\",\n\t\t\"ECONNRESET\",\n\t\t\"ETIMEDOUT\",\n\t}\n\tfor _, p := range transientPatterns {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(combined, p) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (k *Kiro) Bootstrap() error {\n\t// No-op locally. On CI, write config for non-interactive auth if needed.\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (k *Kiro) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error) {\n\tcfg := &runConfig{}\n\tfor _, o := range opts {\n\t\to(cfg)\n\t}\n\n\tbin, err := exec.LookPath(k.Binary())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Output{}, fmt.Errorf(\"%s not in PATH: %w\", k.Binary(), err)\n\t}\n\n\targs := []string{\"chat\", \"--no-interactive\", \"--trust-all-tools\", \"--agent\", \"entire\", prompt}\n\tdisplayArgs := []string{\"chat\", \"--no-interactive\", \"--trust-all-tools\", \"--agent\", \"entire\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%q\", prompt)}\n\n\tenv := filterEnv(os.Environ(), \"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY\")\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, bin, args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tcmd.Env = env\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}\n\tcmd.Cancel = func() error {\n\t\treturn syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)\n\t}\n\tcmd.WaitDelay = 5 * time.Second\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr strings.Builder\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr = cmd.Run()\n\texitCode := 0\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\texitErr := &exec.ExitError{}\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\texitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\texitCode = -1\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn Output{\n\t\tCommand:  k.Binary() + \" \" + strings.Join(displayArgs, \" \"),\n\t\tStdout:   stdout.String(),\n\t\tStderr:   stderr.String(),\n\t\tExitCode: exitCode,\n\t}, err\n}\n\nfunc (k *Kiro) StartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error) {\n\tname := fmt.Sprintf(\"kiro-test-%d\", time.Now().UnixNano())\n\n\ts, err := NewTmuxSession(name, dir, []string{\"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY\"}, k.Binary(), \"chat\", \"--agent\", \"entire\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait for the initial prompt to appear.\n\tif _, err := s.WaitFor(k.PromptPattern(), 30*time.Second); err != nil {\n\t\t_ = s.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"waiting for initial prompt: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\ts.stableAtSend = \"\"\n\n\treturn s, nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/entire/entire.go",
  "content": "package entire\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n)\n\n// BinPath returns the path to the entire binary.\n// It checks E2E_ENTIRE_BIN first, then falls back to looking in PATH.\nfunc BinPath() string {\n\tif p := os.Getenv(\"E2E_ENTIRE_BIN\"); p != \"\" {\n\t\treturn p\n\t}\n\tif p, err := exec.LookPath(\"entire\"); err == nil {\n\t\treturn p\n\t}\n\treturn \"entire\"\n}\n\n// RewindPoint represents a single entry from `entire rewind --list`.\ntype RewindPoint struct {\n\tID             string `json:\"id\"`\n\tMessage        string `json:\"message\"`\n\tMetadataDir    string `json:\"metadata_dir\"`\n\tDate           string `json:\"date\"`\n\tIsLogsOnly     bool   `json:\"is_logs_only\"`\n\tCondensationID string `json:\"condensation_id\"`\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n}\n\n// Enable runs `entire enable` for the given agent with telemetry disabled.\nfunc Enable(t *testing.T, dir, agent string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\trun(t, dir, \"enable\", \"--agent\", agent, \"--telemetry=false\")\n}\n\n// Disable runs `entire disable` in the given directory.\nfunc Disable(t *testing.T, dir string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\trun(t, dir, \"disable\")\n}\n\n// RewindList runs `entire rewind --list` and parses the JSON output.\nfunc RewindList(t *testing.T, dir string) []RewindPoint {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tout := run(t, dir, \"rewind\", \"--list\")\n\n\tvar points []RewindPoint\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(out), &points); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"parse rewind list: %v\\nraw output: %s\", err, out)\n\t}\n\treturn points\n}\n\n// Rewind runs `entire rewind --to <id>`. Returns an error instead of\n// failing the test, since callers may test failure cases.\nfunc Rewind(t *testing.T, dir, id string) error {\n\tt.Helper()\n\treturn runErr(dir, \"rewind\", \"--to\", id)\n}\n\n// RewindLogsOnly runs `entire rewind --to <id> --logs-only`.\nfunc RewindLogsOnly(t *testing.T, dir, id string) error {\n\tt.Helper()\n\treturn runErr(dir, \"rewind\", \"--to\", id, \"--logs-only\")\n}\n\n// run executes an `entire` subcommand in dir and fails the test on error.\nfunc run(t *testing.T, dir string, args ...string) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tcmd := exec.Command(BinPath(), args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), \"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0\")\n\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"entire %s failed: %v\\n%s\", strings.Join(args, \" \"), err, out)\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(out))\n}\n\n// runErr executes an `entire` subcommand in dir and returns any error.\nfunc runErr(dir string, args ...string) error {\n\tcmd := exec.Command(BinPath(), args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), \"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0\")\n\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn &ExecError{\n\t\t\tArgs:   args,\n\t\t\tErr:    err,\n\t\t\tOutput: string(out),\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ExecError wraps an entire CLI execution failure with its output.\ntype ExecError struct {\n\tArgs   []string\n\tErr    error\n\tOutput string\n}\n\nfunc (e *ExecError) Error() string {\n\treturn \"entire \" + strings.Join(e.Args, \" \") + \": \" + e.Err.Error() + \"\\n\" + e.Output\n}\n\nfunc (e *ExecError) Unwrap() error {\n\treturn e.Err\n}\n"
}

`json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\tCacheCreationTokens int `json:\"cache_creation_tokens\"`\n\tCacheReadTokens     int `json:\"cache_read_tokens\"`\n\tOutputTokens        int `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\tAPICallCount        int `json:\"api_call_count\"`\n}\n\n// Attribution tracks code authorship for a checkpoint.\ntype Attribution struct {\n\tCalculatedAt    time.Time `json:\"calculated_at\"`\n\tAgentLines      int       `json:\"agent_lines\"`\n\tHumanAdded      int       `json:\"human_added\"`\n\tHumanModified   int       `json:\"human_modified\"`\n\tHumanRemoved    int       `json:\"human_removed\"`\n\tTotalCommitted  int       `json:\"total_committed\"`\n\tAgentPercentage float64   `json:\"agent_percentage\"`\n}\n\n// CheckpointMetadata represents the top-level metadata.json for a checkpoint.\ntype CheckpointMetadata struct {\n\tCLIVersion       string       `json:\"cli_version\"`\n\tCheckpointID     string       `json:\"checkpoint_id\"`\n\tStrategy         string       `json:\"strategy\"`\n\tBranch           string       `json:\"branch\"`\n\tCheckpointsCount int          `json:\"checkpoints_count\"`\n\tFilesTouched     []string     `json:\"files_touched\"`\n\tSessions         []SessionRef `json:\"sessions\"`\n\tTokenUsage       TokenUsage   `json:\"token_usage\"`\n}\n\n// SessionRef is a pointer to a session within checkpoint metadata.\ntype SessionRef struct {\n\tMetadata    string `json:\"metadata\"`\n\tTranscript  string `json:\"transcript\"`\n\tContext     string `json:\"context\"`\n\tContentHash string `json:\"content_hash\"`\n\tPrompt      string `json:\"prompt\"`\n}\n\n// SessionMetadata represents a session's metadata.json within a checkpoint.\ntype SessionMetadata struct {\n\tCLIVersion         string      `json:\"cli_version\"`\n\tCheckpointID       string      `json:\"checkpoint_id\"`\n\tSessionID          string      `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tStrategy           string      `json:\"strategy\"`\n\tCreatedAt          time.Time   `json:\"created_at\"`\n\tBranch             string      `json:\"branch\"`\n\tAgent              string      `json:\"agent\"`\n\tCheckpointsCount   int         `json:\"checkpoints_count\"`\n\tFilesTouched       []string    `json:\"files_touched\"`\n\tTokenUsage         TokenUsage  `json:\"token_usage\"`\n\tInitialAttribution Attribution `json:\"initial_attribution\"`\n\tTranscriptPath     string      `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n}\n\n// CheckpointPath converts a checkpoint ID to its two-level directory path.\nfunc CheckpointPath(id string) string {\n\treturn id[:2] + \"/\" + id[2:]\n}\n"

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/artifacts.go",
  "content": "package testutil\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// ArtifactRoot is the absolute path to the artifact output directory.\n// Must be set in TestMain before any tests run.\nvar ArtifactRoot string\n\n// ArtifactTimestamp is the timestamp subdirectory for this test run.\nvar ArtifactTimestamp = time.Now().Format(\"2006-01-02T15-04-05\")\n\nvar runDirOverride string\n\n// SetRunDir overrides the artifact run directory (e.g. from E2E_ARTIFACT_DIR).\nfunc SetRunDir(dir string) {\n\trunDirOverride = dir\n}\n\n// ArtifactRunDir returns the directory for the current test run.\nfunc ArtifactRunDir() string {\n\tif runDirOverride != \"\" {\n\t\treturn runDirOverride\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(ArtifactRoot, ArtifactTimestamp)\n}\n\nfunc artifactDir(t *testing.T) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tname := strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), \"/\", \"-\")\n\tdir := filepath.Join(ArtifactRunDir(), name)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"warning: failed to create artifact dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn dir\n}\n\n// CaptureArtifacts captures git state, checkpoint metadata, entire logs,\n// and console output to the artifact directory.\nfunc CaptureArtifacts(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tdir := s.ArtifactDir\n\n\twriteArtifact(t, dir, \"git-log.txt\",\n\t\tgitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"log\", \"--decorate\", \"--graph\", \"--all\"))\n\n\twriteArtifact(t, dir, \"git-tree.txt\",\n\t\tgitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"ls-tree\", \"-r\", \"HEAD\")+\n\t\t\t\"\\n--- entire/checkpoints/v1 ---\\n\"+\n\t\t\tgitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"ls-tree\", \"-r\", \"entire/checkpoints/v1\"))\n\n\t// Capture final pane content from interactive sessions, then close.\n\tif s.session != nil {\n\t\tpane := s.session.Capture()\n\t\twriteArtifact(t, dir, \"pane.txt\", pane)\n\t\t_ = s.session.Close()\n\t}\n\n\tif t.Failed() {\n\t\twriteArtifact(t, dir, \"FAIL\", \"\")\n\t} else {\n\t\twriteArtifact(t, dir, \"PASS\", \"\")\n\t}\n\n\tcaptureCheckpointMetadata(t, s, dir)\n\tcaptureEntireLogs(t, s.Dir, dir)\n\n\tif os.Getenv(\"E2E_KEEP_REPOS\") != \"\" {\n\t\tlink := filepath.Join(dir, \"repo\")\n\t\tif err := os.Symlink(s.Dir, link); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Logf(\"warning: failed to symlink repo: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc captureCheckpointMetadata(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, outDir string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tcommits := NewCheckpointCommits(t, s)\n\tmetaDir := filepath.Join(outDir, \"checkpoint-metadata\")\n\n\tcpIDRe := regexp.MustCompile(`(?m)^Checkpoint:\\s+(\\S+)`)\n\tfor _, sha := range commits {\n\t\tmsg := gitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"log\", \"-1\", \"--format=%B\", sha)\n\t\tm := cpIDRe.FindStringSubmatch(msg)\n\t\tif m == nil || len(m[1]) < 3 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tid := m[1]\n\t\tcpPath := CheckpointPath(id)\n\n\t\tcpDir := filepath.Join(metaDir, cpPath)\n\t\t_ = os.MkdirAll(cpDir, 0o755)\n\t\traw := gitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"show\", sha+\":\"+cpPath+\"/metadata.json\")\n\t\twriteArtifact(t, cpDir, \"metadata.json\", raw)\n\n\t\tfor i := 0; ; i++ {\n\t\t\tsessPath := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%d/metadata.json\", cpPath, i)\n\t\t\traw := gitOutputSafe(s.Dir, \"show\", sha+\":\"+sessPath)\n\t\t\tif raw == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsessDir := filepath.Join(cpDir, strconv.Itoa(i))\n\t\t\t_ = os.MkdirAll(sessDir, 0o755)\n\t\t\twriteArtifact(t, sessDir, \"metadata.json\", raw)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc captureEntireLogs(t *testing.T, repoDir, outDir string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tlogDir := filepath.Join(repoDir, \".entire\", \"logs\")\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(logDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdst := filepath.Join(outDir, \"entire-logs\")\n\t_ = os.MkdirAll(dst, 0o755)\n\tfor _, e := range entries {\n\t\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(logDir, e.Name()))\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\twriteArtifact(t, dst, e.Name(), string(data))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc writeArtifact(t *testing.T, dir, name, content string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tpath := filepath.Join(dir, name)\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"warning: failed to write artifact %s: %v\", path, err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc gitOutputSafe(dir string, args ...string) string {\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tout, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn string(out)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alisha/Projects/external-agents/e2e/testutil/repo.go",
  "content": "package testutil\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/e2e/agents\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/e2e/entire\"\n)\n\n// RepoState holds the working state for a single test's cloned repository.\ntype RepoState struct {\n\tAgent            agents.Agent\n\tDir              string\n\tArtifactDir      string\n\tHeadBefore       string\n\tCheckpointBefore string\n\tConsoleLog       *os.File\n\tsession          agents.Session // interactive session, if started via StartSession\n\tskipArtifacts    bool           // suppresses artifact capture on scenario restart\n}\n\n// SetupRepo creates a fresh git repository in a temporary directory, seeds it\n// with an initial commit, and runs `entire enable` for the given agent.\n// Artifact capture is registered as a cleanup function.\nfunc SetupRepo(t *testing.T, agent agents.Agent) *RepoState {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tkeepRepos := os.Getenv(\"E2E_KEEP_REPOS\") != \"\"\n\n\tdir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"e2e-repo-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"create temp dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif keepRepos {\n\t\tt.Logf(\"E2E_KEEP_REPOS: repo will be preserved at %s\", dir)\n\t} else {\n\t\tt.Cleanup(func() { os.RemoveAll(dir) })\n\t}\n\n\t// Resolve symlinks (macOS: /var -> /private/var) so paths match\n\t// what agent CLIs see when they resolve their own CWD.\n\tif resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(dir); err == nil {\n\t\tdir = resolved\n\t}\n\n\tGit(t, dir, \"init\")\n\tGit(t, dir, \"config\", \"user.name\", \"E2E Test\")\n\tGit(t, dir, \"config\", \"user.email\", \"e2e@test.local\")\n\tGit(t, dir, \"commit\", \"--allow-empty\", \"-m\", \"initial commit\")\n\n\t// External agents need external_agents enabled in settings before enable.\n\tif ea, ok := agent.(agents.ExternalAgent); ok && ea.IsExternalAgent() {\n\t\tentireDir := filepath.Join(dir, \".entire\")\n\t\tif err := os.MkdirAll(entireDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"create .entire for external agent: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(entireDir, \"settings.json\"),\n\t\t\t[]byte(\"{\\\"external_agents\\\": true}\\n\"), 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"write external_agents setting: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tentire.Enable(t, dir, agent.EntireAgent())\n\tPatchSettings(t, dir, map[string]any{\"log_level\": \"debug\"})\n\n\t// Create artifact dir eagerly so console.log is written to disk\n\t// incrementally. Even if the test is killed by a global timeout,\n\t// partial output survives.\n\tartDir := artifactDir(t)\n\tconsoleLog, err := os.Create(filepath.Join(artDir, \"console.log\"))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"create console.log: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tstate := &RepoState{\n\t\tAgent:            agent,\n\t\tDir:              dir,\n\t\tArtifactDir:      artDir,\n\t\tHeadBefore:       GitOutput(t, dir, \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\"),\n\t\tCheckpointBefore: GitOutput(t, dir, \"rev-parse\", \"entire/checkpoints/v1\"),\n\t\tConsoleLog:       consoleLog,\n\t}\n\n\tt.Cleanup(func() {\n\t\t_ = consoleLog.Close()\n\t\tif !state.skipArtifacts {\n\t\t\tCaptureArtifacts(t, state)\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\treturn state\n}\n\n// ForEachAgent runs fn as a parallel subtest for every registered agent.\n// It handles repo setup, concurrency gating, context timeout, and cleanup.\n// The timeout is scaled by each agent's TimeoutMultiplier.\nfunc ForEachAgent(t *testing.T, timeout time.Duration, fn func(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context)) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tt.Parallel()\n\tall := agents.All()\n\tif len(all) == 0 {\n\t\tt.Skip(\"no agents registered (check E2E_AGENT filter)\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, agent := range all {\n\t\tt.Run(agent.Name(), func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\t\tslotCtx := context.Background()\n\t\t\tif deadline, ok := t.Deadline(); ok {\n\t\t\t\tvar cancel context.CancelFunc\n\t\t\t\tslotCtx, cancel = context.WithDeadline(slotCtx, deadline)\n\t\t\t\tdefer cancel()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := agents.AcquireSlot(slotCtx, agent); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"timed out waiting for agent slot: %v\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdefer agents.ReleaseSlot(agent)\n\n\t\t\tscaled := time.Duration(float64(timeout) * agent.TimeoutMultiplier())\n\n\t\t\tvar prevState *RepoState\n\t\t\tfor attempt := range maxScenarioRestarts + 1 {\n\t\t\t\ts := SetupRepo(t, agent)\n\t\t\t\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), scaled)\n\n\t\t\t\tif prevState != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tprevState.skipArtifacts = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\t\trestarted := runScenario(t, s, ctx, fn)\n\t\t\t\tcancel()\n\n\t\t\t\tif !restarted {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tprevState = s\n\t\t\t\tif attempt >= maxScenarioRestarts {\n\t\t\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"exhausted %d scenario attempts due to transient API errors\", maxScenarioRestarts+1)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tt.Logf(\"transient error, restarting scenario (attempt %d/%d)\", attempt+2, maxScenarioRestarts+1)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc runScenario(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context, fn func(t *testing.T, s *RepoState, ctx context.Context)) (restarted bool) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tdefer func() {\n\t\tif r := recover(); r != nil {\n\t\t\tif _, ok := r.(errScenarioRestart); ok {\n\t\t\t\trestarted = true\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpanic(r)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\tfn(t, s, ctx)\n\treturn false\n}\n\nconst maxScenarioRestarts = 2\n\ntype errScenarioRestart struct {\n\tmsg string\n}\n\n// RunPrompt runs an agent prompt, logs the command and output to ConsoleLog,\n// and returns the result. If the agent reports a transient API error, it\n// panics with errScenarioRestart to trigger a full scenario restart.\nfunc (s *RepoState) RunPrompt(t *testing.T, ctx context.Context, prompt string, opts ...agents.Option) (agents.Output, error) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tout, err := s.Agent.RunPrompt(ctx, s.Dir, prompt, opts...)\n\ts.logPromptResult(out)\n\n\tif err != nil && s.Agent.IsTransientError(out, err) {\n\t\terrMsg := fmt.Sprintf(\"transient API error (stderr: %s)\", strings.TrimSpace(out.Stderr))\n\t\tt.Logf(\"%s — restarting scenario\", errMsg)\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(s.ConsoleLog, \"> [transient] %s — restarting scenario\\n\", errMsg)\n\t\tpanic(errScenarioRestart{msg: errMsg})\n\t}\n\n\treturn out, err\n}\n\nfunc (s *RepoState) logPromptResult(out agents.Output) {\n\ts.ConsoleLog.WriteString(\"> \" + out.Command + \"\\n\")\n\ts.ConsoleLog.WriteString(\"stdout:\\n\" + out.Stdout + \"\\n\")\n\ts.ConsoleLog.WriteString(\"stderr:\\n\" + out.Stderr + \"\\n\")\n}\n\n// Git runs a git command in the repo and logs it to ConsoleLog.\nfunc (s *RepoState) Git(t *testing.T, args ...string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\ts.ConsoleLog.WriteString(\"> git \" + strings.Join(args, \" \") + \"\\n\")\n\tGit(t, s.Dir, args...)\n}\n\n// GitOutput runs a git command and returns its trimmed stdout.\nfunc (s *RepoState) GitOutput(t *testing.T, args ...string) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\treturn GitOutput(t, s.Dir, args...)\n}\n\n// StartSession starts an interactive session and registers it for pane\n// capture in artifacts. Returns nil if the agent does not support interactive\n// mode. The session is closed automatically during test cleanup.\nfunc (s *RepoState) StartSession(t *testing.T, ctx context.Context) agents.Session {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tsession, err := s.Agent.StartSession(ctx, s.Dir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"start session: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\ts.session = session\n\treturn session\n}\n\n// WaitFor waits for a pattern in the interactive session's pane and logs the\n// pane content to ConsoleLog after the wait completes.\nfunc (s *RepoState) WaitFor(t *testing.T, session agents.Session, pattern string, timeout time.Duration) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tcontent, err := session.WaitFor(pattern, timeout)\n\tfmt.Fprintf(s.ConsoleLog, \"> pane after WaitFor(%q):\\n%s\\n\", pattern, content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"WaitFor(%q): %v\", pattern, err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// IsExternalAgent returns true if the agent implements the ExternalAgent\n// interface and reports itself as external.\nfunc (s *RepoState) IsExternalAgent() bool {\n\tea, ok := s.Agent.(agents.ExternalAgent)\n\treturn ok && ea.IsExternalAgent()\n}\n\n// Send sends input to an interactive session and logs it to ConsoleLog.\nfunc (s *RepoState) Send(t *testing.T, session agents.Session, input string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\ts.ConsoleLog.WriteString(\"> send: \" + input + \"\\n\")\n\tif err := session.Send(input); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"send failed: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// PatchSettings merges extra keys into .entire/settings.json.\nfunc PatchSettings(t *testing.T, dir string, extra map[string]any) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tpath := filepath.Join(dir, \".entire\", \"settings.json\")\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"read settings: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tvar settings map[string]any\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"parse settings: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tfor k, v := range extra {\n\t\tsettings[k] = v\n\t}\n\tout, err := json.MarshalIndent(settings, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"marshal settings: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(path, out, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"write settings: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// Git runs a git command in the given directory and fails the test on error.\nfunc Git(t *testing.T, dir string, args ...string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", args...)\n\tif dir != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\t}\n\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), \"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=0\")\n\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"git %s failed: %v\\n%s\", strings.Join(args, \" \"), err, out)\n\t}\n}\n\n// GitOutput runs a git command in the given directory, returns its trimmed\n// stdout, and fails the test on error.\nfunc GitOutput(t *testing.T, dir string, args ...string) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", args...)\n\tif dir != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\t}\n\n\tout, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tvar stderr string\n\t\tee := &exec.ExitError{}\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &ee) {\n\t\t\tstderr = string(ee.Stderr)\n\t\t}\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"git %s failed: %v\\n%s\", strings.Join(args, \" \"), err, stderr)\n\t}\n\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(out))\n}\n\n// NewCheckpointCommits returns the SHAs of commits added to the\n// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch since the test was set up, oldest first.\nfunc NewCheckpointCommits(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) []string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tlog := GitOutput(t, s.Dir, \"log\", \"--reverse\", \"--format=%H\", s.CheckpointBefore+\"..entire/checkpoints/v1\")\n\tif log == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Split(log, \"\\n\")\n}\n\n// CheckpointIDs lists all checkpoint IDs from the tree at the tip of the\n// checkpoint branch.\nfunc CheckpointIDs(t *testing.T, dir string) []string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tout := gitOutputSafe(dir, \"ls-tree\", \"-r\", \"--name-only\", \"entire/checkpoints/v1\")\n\tif out == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\tvar ids []string\n\tfor _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(out), \"\\n\") {\n\t\tparts := strings.Split(line, \"/\")\n\t\tif len(parts) == 3 && parts[2] == \"metadata.json\" {\n\t\t\tid := parts[0] + parts[1]\n\t\t\tif !seen[id] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[id] = true\n\t\t\t\tids = append(ids, id)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn ids\n}\n\n// ReadCheckpointMetadata reads checkpoint-level metadata.json from the\n// checkpoint branch for the given checkpoint ID.\nfunc ReadCheckpointMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string) CheckpointMetadata {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tpath := CheckpointPath(checkpointID) + \"/metadata.json\"\n\tblob := \"entire/checkpoints/v1:\" + path\n\n\traw := GitOutput(t, dir, \"show\", blob)\n\n\tvar meta CheckpointMetadata\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal checkpoint metadata from %s: %v\", blob, err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn meta\n}\n\n// ReadSessionMetadata reads a session's metadata.json from the checkpoint\n// branch for the given checkpoint ID and session index.\nfunc ReadSessionMetadata(t *testing.T, dir string, checkpointID string, sessionIndex int) SessionMetadata {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tpath := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/%d/metadata.json\", CheckpointPath(checkpointID), sessionIndex)\n\tblob := \"entire/checkpoints/v1:\" + path\n\n\traw := GitOutput(t, dir, \"show\", blob)\n\n\tvar meta SessionMetadata\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &meta); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unmarshal session metadata from %s: %v\", blob, err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn meta\n}\n\n// SetupBareRemote creates a bare git repo, adds it as \"origin\", and pushes\n// the initial commit. Returns the bare repo path.\nfunc SetupBareRemote(t *testing.T, s *RepoState) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\tvar bareDir string\n\tif os.Getenv(\"E2E_KEEP_REPOS\") != \"\" {\n\t\tvar err error\n\t\tbareDir, err = os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"e2e-bare-*\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"create bare remote dir: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tt.Logf(\"E2E_KEEP_REPOS: bare remote will be preserved at %s\", bareDir)\n\t} else {\n\t\tbareDir = t.TempDir()\n\t}\n\n\tGit(t, bareDir, \"init\", \"--bare\")\n\tGit(t, s.Dir, \"remote\", \"add\", \"origin\", bareDir)\n\tGit(t, s.Dir, \"push\", \"-u\", \"origin\", \"HEAD\")\n\treturn bareDir\n}\n\n// GitOutputErr runs a git command and returns (output, error) without\n// failing the test.\nfunc GitOutputErr(dir string, args ...string) (string, error) {\n\tcmd := exec.Command(git, args...)\n\tif dir != \"\" {\n\t\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\t}\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(string(out)), err\n}\n\n// GetCheckpointTrailer extracts the Entire-Checkpoint trailer value from a\n// code commit. Returns the trimmed trailer value, or an empty string if the\n// trailer is not present.\nfunc GetCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, dir string, ref string) string {\n\tt.Helper()\n\n\treturn GitOutput(t, dir, \"log\", \"-1\", \"--format=%(trailers:key=Entire-Checkpoint,valueonly)\", ref)\n}\n"
}

  "content": "//go:build e2e\n\npackage e2e\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"runtime\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\n\t// Import agents package to trigger init() registration.\n\t_ \"github.com/entireio/external-agents/e2e/agents\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/e2e/entire\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/e2e/testutil\"\n)\n\n// agentBinaries maps agent names to their built binary paths.\nvar agentBinaries = map[string]string{}\n\nfunc TestMain(m *testing.M) {\n\ttmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(\"\", \"e2e-agents-*\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"failed to create temp dir: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\tos.Exit(1)\n\t}\n\tdefer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)\n\n\tagents, err := discoverAgents()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"failed to discover agents: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\tos.Exit(1)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(agents) == 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, \"no agents found in agents/ directory\")\n\t\tos.Exit(1)\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, agentDir := range agents {\n\t\tagentName := filepath.Base(agentDir)\n\t\tbinPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, agentName)\n\t\t// Each agent has its own go.mod, so build from the agent's directory\n\t\tagentAbsDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot(), agentDir)\n\t\tmainPkg := \"./cmd/\" + agentName\n\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Building %s...\\n\", agentName)\n\t\tcmd := exec.Command(\"go\", \"build\", \"-o\", binPath, mainPkg)\n\t\tcmd.Dir = agentAbsDir\n\t\tcmd.Stdout = os.Stdout\n\t\tcmd.Stderr = os.Stderr\n\t\tif err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"failed to build %s: %v\\n\", agentName, err)\n\t\t\tos.Exit(1)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tagentBinaries[agentName] = binPath\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"Built %s -> %s\\n\", agentName, binPath)\n\t}\n\n\t// Add the temp bin directory to PATH so that `entire enable` can discover\n\t// the agent binaries (e.g. entire-agent-kiro) during lifecycle tests.\n\tos.Setenv(\"PATH\", tmpDir+string(os.PathListSeparator)+os.Getenv(\"PATH\"))\n\n\t// --- Artifact directory setup ---\n\trunDir := os.Getenv(\"E2E_ARTIFACT_DIR\")\n\tif runDir == \"\" {\n\t\t_, file, _, _ := runtime.Caller(0)\n\t\ttestutil.ArtifactRoot = filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(file), \"artifacts\")\n\t\trunDir = testutil.ArtifactRunDir()\n\t}\n\t_ = os.MkdirAll(runDir, 0o755)\n\ttestutil.SetRunDir(runDir)\n\n\t// Prepend the entire binary's directory to PATH so git hooks resolve\n\t// to the same binary the test harness uses.\n\tentireBin := entire.BinPath()\n\tif dir := filepath.Dir(entireBin); dir != \".\" {\n\t\tos.Setenv(\"PATH\", dir+string(os.PathListSeparator)+os.Getenv(\"PATH\"))\n\t}\n\n\t// --- Preflight checks ---\n\tif _, err := exec.LookPath(\"tmux\"); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, \"warning: tmux not found — interactive session tests will fail\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Write entire version info to artifact dir.\n\tversion := \"unknown\"\n\tif out, err := exec.Command(entireBin, \"version\").Output(); err == nil {\n\t\tversion = string(out)\n\t}\n\tpreflight := fmt.Sprintf(\"entire binary:  %s\\nentire version: %s\\n\", entireBin, version)\n\t_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(runDir, \"entire-version.txt\"), []byte(preflight), 0o644)\n\n\t// Isolate git config to prevent user's ~/.gitconfig from interfering.\n\tos.Setenv(\"GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL\", \"/dev/null\")\n\n\tos.Exit(m.Run())\n}\n\nfunc discoverAgents() ([]string, error) {\n\tagentsDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot(), \"agents\")\n\tentries, err := os.ReadDir(agentsDir)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"read agents dir: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar agentDirs []string\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif !entry.IsDir() {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name(), \"entire-agent-\") {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Verify it has a cmd/<name>/main.go\n\t\tmainFile := filepath.Join(agentsDir, entry.Name(), \"cmd\", entry.Name(), \"main.go\")\n\t\tif _, err := os.Stat(mainFile); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tagentDirs = append(agentDirs, filepath.Join(\"agents\", entry.Name()))\n\t}\n\treturn agentDirs, nil\n}\n\nfunc repoRoot() string {\n\t// Walk up from e2e/ to find the repo root\n\tdir, err := os.Getwd()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"..\"\n\t}\n\t// If we're in the e2e directory, go up one level\n\tif filepath.Base(dir) == \"e2e\" {\n\t\treturn filepath.Dir(dir)\n\t}\n\treturn dir\n}\n"
}

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
